Implement the following plan:

# Fix WeTTY Terminal: Get it working in local Docker

## Context
WeTTY terminal implementation is complete (code, tests, UI all done). The blocker is the local Docker networking: Traefik needs to route `/terminal/{id}/wetty` to the WeTTY sidecar container, but the local setup is missing Traefik and has network mismatches.

## Root Causes Found
1. **`inject_pubkey` hangs** — `container.exec` with `stdin: StringIO` never sends EOF → fixed with base64 pipe approach
2. **No Traefik locally** — production has Traefik as a Kamal accessory; local Docker Compose doesn't
3. **Network mismatch** — `sandcastle-web` container is on `sandcastle_default` network, not `sandcastle-web` network where Traefik + WeTTY live
4. **TLS mode** — dynamic configs need `SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE=selfsigned` for local dev (no Let's Encrypt)

## Plan
1. Connect `sandcastle-web` to `sandcastle-web` Docker network (so Traefik can reach Rails)
2. Verify Traefik container (`sandcastle-traefik`) can reach `sandcastle-web:80`
3. Set `SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE=selfsigned` in the running Rails container and restart
4. Regenerate dynamic Traefik configs in self-signed mode
5. Verify WeTTY container is running (restart if needed)
6. Open `https://localhost:8443` in Chrome, click Terminal, verify WeTTY shell appears
7. If anything fails, check `docker logs` and fix iteratively

## Files to touch
- `app/services/terminal_manager.rb` — already fixed (base64 inject_pubkey)
- Docker runtime config only (no code changes expected)


If you need specific details from before exiting plan mode (like exact code snippets, error messages, or content you generated), read the full transcript at: /Users/thies/.REDACTED.jsonl

     7→  class Error < StandardError; end

     9→  # Opens a web terminal for the given sandbox.
    10→  # Returns the URL path to the WeTTY session.
    11→  def open(sandbox:)
    12→    raise Error, "Sandbox is not running" unless sandbox.status == "running"
    13→    raise Error, "Sandbox has no container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
    14→
    15→    user = sandbox.user
    16→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)

    35→    raise Error, "Failed to open terminal: #{e.message}"

    37→
    38→  # Closes the web terminal for the given sandbox.
    39→  def close(sandbox:)
    40→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
    41→
    42→    # Stop and remove WeTTY container
    43→    begin

    69→  # Removes orphaned WeTTY containers whose sandbox no longer exists or is not running.
    70→  def cleanup_orphaned
    71→    Docker::Container.all(all: true).each do |container|
    72→      name = container.info.dig("Names")&.first&.delete_prefix("/")
    73→      next unless name&.start_with?("sc-wetty-")
    74→
    75→      labels = container.info["Labels"] || {}
    76→      sandbox_id = labels["sandcastle.sandbox_id"]&.to_i
    77→
    78→      sandbox = sandbox_id ? Sandbox.find_by(id: sandbox_id) : nil
    79→      should_remove = sandbox.nil? || sandbox.status != "running"
    80→
    81→      if should_remove
    82→        container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    83→        container.delete(force: true)
    84→        Rails.logger.info("TerminalManager: removed orphaned WeTTY container #{name}")
    85→
    86→        # Clean up Traefik config and keys if we have a sandbox_id
    87→        if sandbox_id
    88→          config_path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{sandbox_id}.yml")
    89→          File.delete(config_path) if File.exist?(config_path)
    90→        end
    91→
    92→        # Extract full_name from container name and validate format
    93→        full_name = name.delete_prefix("sc-wetty-")
    94→        unless full_name.match?(/\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]+-[a-z][a-z0-9_-]*\z/)
    95→          Rails.logger.warn("TerminalManager: skipping suspicious container name: #{name}")
    96→          next
    97→        end
    98→
    99→        key_dir = File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty", full_name)
   100→        expected_parent = File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty")
   101→        unless File.expand_path(key_dir).start_with?("#{File.expand_path(expected_parent)}/")
   102→          Rails.logger.warn("TerminalManager: path traversal attempt detected: #{key_dir}")
   103→          next
   104→        end
   105→
   106→        FileUtils.rm_rf(key_dir) if Dir.exist?(key_dir)
   107→      end
   108→    end
   109→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   110→    Rails.logger.error("TerminalManager: orphan cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
   111→  end
   112→
   113→  private
   114→
   115→  def wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   116→    "sc-wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}"
   117→  end
   118→
   119→  def wetty_url(sandbox)
   120→    "/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty"
   121→  end
   122→
   123→  def key_dir_path(sandbox)
   124→    File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty", sandbox.full_name)
   125→  end
   126→
   127→  def container_running?(name)
   128→    container = Docker::Container.get(name)
   129→    container.json.dig("State", "Running") == true
   130→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   131→    false
   132→  end
   133→
   134→  def pull_image
   135→    Docker::Image.get(WETTY_IMAGE)
   136→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   137→    Docker::Image.create("fromImage" => WETTY_IMAGE)
   138→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   139→    raise Error, "Failed to pull #{WETTY_IMAGE} — check network connectivity"
   140→  end
   141→
   142→  def ensure_network
   143→    Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   144→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   145→    Docker::Network.create(NETWORK_NAME, "Driver" => "bridge")
   146→  end
   147→
   148→  def connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
   149→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   150→
   151→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   152→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   153→
   154→    networks = container.json.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks") || {}
   155→    return if networks.key?(NETWORK_NAME)
   156→
   157→    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)
   158→  end
   159→
   160→  def generate_keypair(sandbox)
   161→    key_dir = key_dir_path(sandbox)
   162→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(key_dir, mode: 0o700)
   163→
   164→    key_path = File.join(key_dir, "key")
   165→    # Remove old keys if they exist
   166→    FileUtils.rm_f(key_path)
   167→    FileUtils.rm_f("#{key_path}.pub")
   168→
   169→    system("ssh-keygen", "-t", "ed25519", "-f", key_path, "-N", "", "-q", "-C", "wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}",
   170→      exception: true)
   171→
   172→    # Ensure private key is only readable by owner
   173→    File.chmod(0o600, key_path)
   174→
   175→    key_dir
   176→  end
   177→
   178→  def inject_pubkey(sandbox, key_dir)
   179→    pubkey = File.read(File.join(key_dir, "key.pub")).strip
   180→    username = sandbox.user.name
   181→
   182→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   183→
   184→    # Write pubkey via base64 to avoid any shell-injection risk.
   185→    # The stdin: approach hangs because docker-api never sends EOF to cat.
   186→    encoded = Base64.strict_encode64("#{pubkey}\n")
   187→    container.exec([ "mkdir", "-p", "/home/#{username}/.ssh" ])
   188→    container.exec([ "sh", "-c", "echo #{encoded} | base64 -d >> /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys" ])
   189→    container.exec([ "chown", "-R", "#{username}:#{username}", "/home/#{username}/.ssh" ])
   190→    container.exec([ "chmod", "600", "/home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys" ])
   191→  end
   192→
   193→  def create_wetty_container(sandbox:, user:, key_dir:)
   194→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   195→
   196→    # Remove any existing container with this name
   197→    begin
   198→      old = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
   199→      old.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
   200→      old.delete(force: true)
   201→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   202→      # No existing container
   203→    end
   204→
   205→    key_path = File.join(key_dir, "key")
   206→
   207→    container = Docker::Container.create(
   208→      "name" => container_name,
   209→      "Image" => WETTY_IMAGE,
   210→      "Env" => [
   211→        "SSHHOST=#{sandbox.full_name}",
   212→        "SSHPORT=22",
   213→        "SSHUSER=#{user.name}",
   214→        "SSHAUTH=publickey",
   215→        "SSHKEY=/etc/wetty/key",
   216→        "BASE=/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty"
   217→      ],
   218→      "HostConfig" => {
   219→        "NetworkMode" => NETWORK_NAME,
   220→        "Binds" => [ "#{key_path}:/etc/wetty/key:ro" ],
   221→        "RestartPolicy" => { "Name" => "no" },
   222→        "Memory" => 128 * 1024 * 1024, # 128MB
   223→        "NanoCpus" => 500_000_000 # 0.5 CPU
   224→      },
   225→      "Labels" => {
   226→        "sandcastle.sandbox_id" => sandbox.id.to_s,
   227→        "sandcastle.role" => "wetty"
   228→      }
   229→    )
   230→
   231→    container.start
   232→    container
   233→  end
   234→
   235→  def write_traefik_config(sandbox)
   236→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
   237→
   238→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   239→    id = sandbox.id
   240→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   241→
   242→    rule = if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   243→      "HostRegexp(`.+`) && PathPrefix(`/terminal/#{id}/wetty`)"
   244→    else
   245→      "Host(`#{host}`) && PathPrefix(`/terminal/#{id}/wetty`)"
   246→    end
   247→
   248→    config = {
   249→      "http" => {
   250→        "routers" => {
   251→          "terminal-#{id}" => {
   252→            "rule" => rule,
   253→            "service" => "terminal-#{id}",
   254→            "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
   255→            "tls" => tls_config,
   256→            "middlewares" => [ "terminal-auth-#{id}" ],
   257→            "priority" => 100
   258→          }
   259→        },
   260→        "middlewares" => {
   261→          "terminal-auth-#{id}" => {
   262→            "forwardAuth" => {
   263→              "address" => "http://sandcastle-web:80/terminal/auth",
   264→              "trustForwardHeader" => true
   265→            }
   266→          }
   267→        },
   268→        "services" => {
   269→          "terminal-#{id}" => {
   270→            "loadBalancer" => {
   271→              "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{container_name}:3000" } ]
   272→            }
   273→          }
   274→        }
   275→      }
   276→    }
   277→
   278→    File.write(File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{id}.yml"), config.to_yaml)
   279→  end
   280→
   281→  def tls_config
   282→    if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   283→      {}
   284→    else
   285→      { "certResolver" => "letsencrypt" }
   286→    end
   287→  end
   288→
   289→  def delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
   290→    path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{sandbox.id}.yml")
   291→    File.delete(path) if File.exist?(path)
   292→  end
   293→
   294→  def remove_pubkey(sandbox)
   295→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   296→
   297→    begin
   298→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   299→      username = sandbox.user.name
   300→      marker = "wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}"
   301→      # Use grep -vF (fixed string) to avoid regex injection via sed
   302→      container.exec([
   303→        "sh", "-c",
   304→        "grep -vF '#{marker}' /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys > /tmp/ak_clean && " \
   305→        "mv /tmp/ak_clean /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys || true"
   306→      ])
   307→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError, Docker::Error::DockerError
   308→      # Sandbox container gone or exec failed — best-effort
   309→    end
   310→  end
   311→end
   312→

     1→class TerminalController < ApplicationController
     2→  allow_unauthenticated_access only: :auth
     3→  skip_before_action :require_password_change, only: :auth
     4→
     5→  def open
     6→    sandbox = if Current.user.admin?
     7→      Sandbox.active.find(params[:id])
     8→    else
     9→      Current.user.sandboxes.active.find(params[:id])
    10→    end
    11→
    12→    url = TerminalManager.new.open(sandbox: sandbox)
    13→    redirect_to url, allow_other_host: false, status: :see_other
    14→  rescue TerminalManager::Error => e
    15→    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message
    16→  end
    17→
    18→  def close
    19→    sandbox = if Current.user.admin?
    20→      Sandbox.active.find(params[:id])
    21→    else
    22→      Current.user.sandboxes.active.find(params[:id])
    23→    end
    24→
    25→    TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
    26→    redirect_to root_path, notice: "Terminal closed"
    27→  rescue TerminalManager::Error => e
    28→    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message
    29→  end
    30→
    31→  # Called by Traefik forwardAuth — returns status code only, no body/redirect
    32→  def auth
    33→    forwarded_uri = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Uri"] || ""
    34→    match = forwarded_uri.match(%r{/terminal/(\d+)/wetty})
    35→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless match
    36→
    37→    session_record = find_session_by_cookie
    38→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless session_record
    39→
    40→    user = session_record.user
    41→    sandbox = Sandbox.active.find_by(id: match[1].to_i)
    42→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless sandbox && (sandbox.user_id == user.id || user.admin?)
    43→
    44→    head :ok
    45→  end
    46→end
    47→

id: toolu_01MPEFymEeCsz4G5XFDYgTyp
```
class SessionsController < ApplicationController
  allow_unauthenticated_access only: %i[ new create ]
  skip_before_action :require_password_change
  rate_limit to: 10, within: 3.minutes, only: :create, with: -> { redirect_to new_session_path, alert: "Try again later." }

  def new
  end

  def create
    if user = User.authenticate_by(params.permit(:email_address, :password))
      start_new_session_for user
      redirect_to after_authentication_url
    else
      redirect_to new_session_path, alert: "Try another email address or password."
    end
  end

  def destroy
    terminate_session
    redirect_to new_session_path, status: :see_other
  end
end
```

> AGENT

Found the problem! The SSH key is mounted as a directory instead of a file, and permissions are 0755. This is a Docker volume path resolution issue — the Rails container runs inside Docker and uses `/data` (a volume), but bind mounts in new containers resolve against the Docker host filesystem. Let me check how SandboxManager handles this.

> AGENT

Found the root cause! The key file at `/data/wetty/thies-hello/key` exists inside the Docker volume but not on the host filesystem. When Docker creates the WeTTY bind mount, it looks on the host where the file doesn't exist, so it creates a directory instead.

I need to fix `TerminalManager` to share the Docker volume instead of using a direct bind mount. Let me check how the Rails container's volume is configured.

> TOOL

> AGENT

The key issue: the Rails container writes keys inside a Docker volume at `/data`, but Docker bind-mounts resolve against the host filesystem, not the container's filesystem. I need to fix `TerminalManager` to share the volume properly.

Let me implement the fix by copying the key into the WeTTY container after creation using Docker's `archive_in` API, which avoids the volume path resolution problem entirely.

> TOOL

```
   193→  def create_wetty_container(sandbox:, user:, key_dir:)
   194→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   195→
   196→    # Remove any existing container with this name
   197→    begin
   198→      old = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
   199→      old.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
   200→      old.delete(force: true)
   201→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   202→      # No existing container
   203→    end
   204→
   205→    key_path = File.join(key_dir, "key")
   206→
   207→    container = Docker::Container.create(
   208→      "name" => container_name,
   209→      "Image" => WETTY_IMAGE,
   210→      "Env" => [
   211→        "SSHHOST=#{sandbox.full_name}",
   212→        "SSHPORT=22",
   213→        "SSHUSER=#{user.name}",
   214→        "SSHAUTH=publickey",

> AGENT

Now let me implement the fix. Instead of bind-mounting the key file (which fails when `/data` is a Docker volume), I'll copy the key into the WeTTY container after creation using `archive_in`.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Wc4F2TuHNMggWpdhRKfczG
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/terminal_manager.rb",
  "old_string": "  def create_wetty_container(sandbox:, user:, key_dir:)\n    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)\n\n    # Remove any existing container with this name\n    begin\n      old = Docker::Container.get(container_name)\n      old.stop(t: 3) rescue nil\n      old.delete(force: true)\n    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n      # No existing container\n    end\n\n    key_path = File.join(key_dir, \"key\")\n\n    container = Docker::Container.create(\n      \"name\" => container_name,\n      \"Image\" => WETTY_IMAGE,\n      \"Env\" => [\n        \"SSHHOST=#{sandbox.full_name}\",\n        \"SSHPORT=22\",\n        \"SSHUSER=#{user.name}\",\n        \"SSHAUTH=publickey\",\n        \"SSHKEY=/etc/wetty/key\",\n        \"BASE=/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty\"\n      ],\n      \"HostConfig\" => {\n        \"NetworkMode\" => NETWORK_NAME,\n        \"Binds\" => [ \"#{key_path}:/etc/wetty/key:ro\" ],\n        \"RestartPolicy\" => { \"Name\" => \"no\" },\n        \"Memory\" => 128 * 1024 * 1024, # 128MB\n        \"NanoCpus\" => 500_000_000 # 0.5 CPU\n      },\n      \"Labels\" => {\n        \"sandcastle.sandbox_id\" => sandbox.id.to_s,\n        \"sandcastle.role\" => \"wetty\"\n      }\n    )\n\n    container.start\n    container\n  end",
  "new_string": "  def create_wetty_container(sandbox:, user:, key_dir:)\n    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)\n\n    # Remove any existing container with this name\n    begin\n      old = Docker::Container.get(container_name)\n      old.stop(t: 3) rescue nil\n      old.delete(force: true)\n    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n      # No existing container\n    end\n\n    container = Docker::Container.create(\n      \"name\" => container_name,\n      \"Image\" => WETTY_IMAGE,\n      \"Env\" => [\n        \"SSHHOST=#{sandbox.full_name}\",\n        \"SSHPORT=22\",\n        \"SSHUSER=#{user.name}\",\n        \"SSHAUTH=publickey\",\n        \"SSHKEY=/etc/wetty/key\",\n        \"BASE=/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty\"\n      ],\n      \"HostConfig\" => {\n        \"NetworkMode\" => NETWORK_NAME,\n        \"RestartPolicy\" => { \"Name\" => \"no\" },\n        \"Memory\" => 128 * 1024 * 1024, # 128MB\n        \"NanoCpus\" => 500_000_000 # 0.5 CPU\n      },\n      \"Labels\" => {\n        \"sandcastle.sandbox_id\" => sandbox.id.to_s,\n        \"sandcastle.role\" => \"wetty\"\n      }\n    )\n\n    container.start\n    copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)\n    container\n  end\n\n  # Copy the SSH private key into the WeTTY container via Docker archive API.\n  # This avoids bind-mount path issues when Rails runs inside a container\n  # with a Docker volume for /data (the host path doesn't match).\n  def copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)\n    key_content = File.read(File.join(key_dir, \"key\"))\n\n    tar = StringIO.new\n    tar.set_encoding(\"BINARY\")\n    Gem::Package::TarWriter.new(tar) do |tw|\n      tw.add_file_simple(\"key\", 0o600, key_content.bytesize) do |io|\n        io.write(key_content)\n      end\n    end\n    tar.rewind\n\n    container.archive_in_stream(\"/etc/wetty/\") { tar.string }\n  end"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Qdmg48LcUNSBt7ycAZB8hf
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/terminal_manager.rb",
  "old_string": "  # Copy the SSH private key into the WeTTY container via Docker archive API.\n  # This avoids bind-mount path issues when Rails runs inside a container\n  # with a Docker volume for /data (the host path doesn't match).\n  def copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)\n    key_content = File.read(File.join(key_dir, \"key\"))\n\n    tar = StringIO.new\n    tar.set_encoding(\"BINARY\")\n    Gem::Package::TarWriter.new(tar) do |tw|\n      tw.add_file_simple(\"key\", 0o600, key_content.bytesize) do |io|\n        io.write(key_content)\n      end\n    end\n    tar.rewind\n\n    container.archive_in_stream(\"/etc/wetty/\") { tar.string }\n  end",
  "new_string": "  # Copy the SSH private key into the WeTTY container via exec + base64.\n  # This avoids bind-mount path issues when Rails runs inside a container\n  # with a Docker volume for /data (the host path doesn't match).\n  def copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)\n    key_content = File.read(File.join(key_dir, \"key\"))\n    encoded = Base64.strict_encode64(key_content)\n\n    container.exec([ \"mkdir\", \"-p\", \"/etc/wetty\" ])\n    container.exec([ \"sh\", \"-c\", \"echo #{encoded} | base64 -d > /etc/wetty/key\" ])\n    container.exec([ \"chmod\", \"600\", \"/etc/wetty/key\" ])\n  end"
}
```

> AGENT

The Chrome extension is disconnected. Let me summarize the current state and what you can test manually.

Here's the status:

**What's working (verified via curl):**
1. Traefik routes `https://localhost:8443/terminal/7/wetty` → WeTTY container (200 OK)
2. ForwardAuth checks session cookie → returns 200 for authenticated users, 401 for unauthenticated
3. WeTTY HTML page loads correctly with base path `/terminal/7/wetty`
4. Socket.IO polling endpoint works (200)
5. SSH from WeTTY container to sandbox works (`thies@thies-hello` via publickey)
6. Key file has correct permissions (600) in WeTTY container

**To test manually in Chrome:**
1. Navigate to `https://localhost:8443`
2. Log in with `admin@sandcastle.rocks` / `sandcastle`
3. Click the Terminal button on the `thies-hello` sandbox
4. You should see a WeTTY terminal shell

**What was fixed:**
1. **`inject_pubkey` hang** — switched from `stdin: StringIO` to base64 pipe approach (already in your code from previous session)
2. **Key mount failure** — replaced bind-mount (`"Binds" => ["key_path:/etc/wetty/key:ro"]`) with `container.exec` + base64 copy. Bind mounts fail when Rails runs inside a container with a Docker volume for `/data`, because Docker resolves bind paths against the host filesystem, not the container's.
3. **docker-compose.local.yml** — added `SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE: selfsigned` and `sandcastle-web` external network so Rails joins the Traefik network automatically on `docker compose up`.

Can you reconnect Chrome (maybe restart it) so I can do a visual test, or would you like to test manually?

     7→  class Error < StandardError; end

     9→  # Opens a web terminal for the given sandbox.
    10→  # Returns the URL path to the WeTTY session.
    11→  def open(sandbox:)
    12→    raise Error, "Sandbox is not running" unless sandbox.status == "running"
    13→    raise Error, "Sandbox has no container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
    14→
    15→    user = sandbox.user
    16→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
    17→

    21→    end
    22→
    23→    pull_image

    32→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    33→    raise Error, "Failed to open terminal: #{e.message}"
    34→  rescue SystemCallError => e
    35→    raise Error, "Failed to open terminal: #{e.message}"
    36→  end
    37→
    38→  # Closes the web terminal for the given sandbox.
    39→  def close(sandbox:)
    40→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
    41→
    42→    # Stop and remove WeTTY container
    43→    begin
    44→      container = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
    45→      container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    46→      container.delete(force: true)
    47→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
    48→      # Already gone
    49→    end
    50→
    51→    # Delete Traefik config
    52→    delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
    53→
    54→    # Clean up key directory
    55→    key_dir = key_dir_path(sandbox)

    60→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e

    63→
    64→  # Returns true if the WeTTY container is running for this sandbox.
    65→  def active?(sandbox:)
    66→    container_running?(wetty_container_name(sandbox))
    67→  end
    68→
    69→  # Removes orphaned WeTTY containers whose sandbox no longer exists or is not running.
    70→  def cleanup_orphaned
    71→    Docker::Container.all(all: true).each do |container|
    72→      name = container.info.dig("Names")&.first&.delete_prefix("/")
    73→      next unless name&.start_with?("sc-wetty-")
    74→
    75→      labels = container.info["Labels"] || {}
    76→      sandbox_id = labels["sandcastle.sandbox_id"]&.to_i
    77→
    78→      sandbox = sandbox_id ? Sandbox.find_by(id: sandbox_id) : nil
    79→      should_remove = sandbox.nil? || sandbox.status != "running"
    80→
    81→      if should_remove
    82→        container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    83→        container.delete(force: true)
    84→        Rails.logger.info("TerminalManager: removed orphaned WeTTY container #{name}")
    85→
    86→        # Clean up Traefik config and keys if we have a sandbox_id
    87→        if sandbox_id
    88→          config_path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{sandbox_id}.yml")
    89→          File.delete(config_path) if File.exist?(config_path)
    90→        end
    91→
    92→        # Extract full_name from container name and validate format
    93→        full_name = name.delete_prefix("sc-wetty-")
    94→        unless full_name.match?(/\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]+-[a-z][a-z0-9_-]*\z/)
    95→          Rails.logger.warn("TerminalManager: skipping suspicious container name: #{name}")
    96→          next
    97→        end
    98→
    99→        key_dir = File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty", full_name)
   100→        expected_parent = File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty")
   101→        unless File.expand_path(key_dir).start_with?("#{File.expand_path(expected_parent)}/")
   102→          Rails.logger.warn("TerminalManager: path traversal attempt detected: #{key_dir}")
   103→          next
   104→        end
   105→
   106→        FileUtils.rm_rf(key_dir) if Dir.exist?(key_dir)
   107→      end
   108→    end
   109→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   110→    Rails.logger.error("TerminalManager: orphan cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
   111→  end
   112→
   113→  private
   114→
   115→  def wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   116→    "sc-wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}"
   117→  end
   118→
   119→  def wetty_url(sandbox)
   120→    "/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty"
   121→  end
   122→
   123→  def key_dir_path(sandbox)
   124→    File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty", sandbox.full_name)
   125→  end
   126→
   127→  def container_running?(name)
   128→    container = Docker::Container.get(name)
   129→    container.json.dig("State", "Running") == true
   130→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   131→    false
   132→  end
   133→
   134→  def pull_image
   135→    Docker::Image.get(WETTY_IMAGE)
   136→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   137→    Docker::Image.create("fromImage" => WETTY_IMAGE)
   138→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   139→    raise Error, "Failed to pull #{WETTY_IMAGE} — check network connectivity"
   140→  end
   141→
   142→  def ensure_network
   143→    Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   144→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   145→    Docker::Network.create(NETWORK_NAME, "Driver" => "bridge")
   146→  end
   147→
   148→  def connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
   149→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   150→
   151→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   152→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   153→
   154→    networks = container.json.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks") || {}
   155→    return if networks.key?(NETWORK_NAME)
   156→
   157→    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)
   158→  end
   159→
   160→  def generate_keypair(sandbox)
   161→    key_dir = key_dir_path(sandbox)
   162→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(key_dir, mode: 0o700)
   163→
   164→    key_path = File.join(key_dir, "key")
   165→    # Remove old keys if they exist
   166→    FileUtils.rm_f(key_path)
   167→    FileUtils.rm_f("#{key_path}.pub")
   168→
   169→    system("ssh-keygen", "-t", "ed25519", "-f", key_path, "-N", "", "-q", "-C", "wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}",
   170→      exception: true)
   171→
   172→    # Ensure private key is only readable by owner
   173→    File.chmod(0o600, key_path)
   174→
   175→    key_dir
   176→  end
   177→
   178→  def inject_pubkey(sandbox, key_dir)
   179→    pubkey = File.read(File.join(key_dir, "key.pub")).strip
   180→    username = sandbox.user.name
   181→
   182→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   183→
   184→    # Write pubkey via base64 to avoid any shell-injection risk.
   185→    # The stdin: approach hangs because docker-api never sends EOF to cat.
   186→    encoded = Base64.strict_encode64("#{pubkey}\n")
   187→    container.exec([ "mkdir", "-p", "/home/#{username}/.ssh" ])
   188→    container.exec([ "sh", "-c", "echo #{encoded} | base64 -d >> /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys" ])
   189→    container.exec([ "chown", "-R", "#{username}:#{username}", "/home/#{username}/.ssh" ])
   190→    container.exec([ "chmod", "600", "/home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys" ])
   191→  end
   192→
   193→  def create_wetty_container(sandbox:, user:, key_dir:)
   194→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   195→
   196→    # Remove any existing container with this name
   197→    begin
   198→      old = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
   199→      old.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
   200→      old.delete(force: true)
   201→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   202→      # No existing container
   203→    end
   204→
   205→    container = Docker::Container.create(
   206→      "name" => container_name,
   207→      "Image" => WETTY_IMAGE,
   208→      "Env" => [
   209→        "SSHHOST=#{sandbox.full_name}",
   210→        "SSHPORT=22",
   211→        "SSHUSER=#{user.name}",
   212→        "SSHAUTH=publickey",
   213→        "SSHKEY=/etc/wetty/key",
   214→        "BASE=/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty"
   215→      ],
   216→      "HostConfig" => {
   217→        "NetworkMode" => NETWORK_NAME,
   218→        "RestartPolicy" => { "Name" => "no" },
   219→        "Memory" => 128 * 1024 * 1024, # 128MB
   220→        "NanoCpus" => 500_000_000 # 0.5 CPU
   221→      },
   222→      "Labels" => {
   223→        "sandcastle.sandbox_id" => sandbox.id.to_s,
   224→        "sandcastle.role" => "wetty"
   225→      }
   226→    )
   227→
   228→    container.start
   229→    copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)
   230→    container
   231→  end
   232→
   233→  # Copy the SSH private key into the WeTTY container via exec + base64.
   234→  # This avoids bind-mount path issues when Rails runs inside a container
   235→  # with a Docker volume for /data (the host path doesn't match).
   236→  def copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)
   237→    key_content = File.read(File.join(key_dir, "key"))
   238→    encoded = Base64.strict_encode64(key_content)
   239→
   240→    container.exec([ "mkdir", "-p", "/etc/wetty" ])
   241→    container.exec([ "sh", "-c", "echo #{encoded} | base64 -d > /etc/wetty/key" ])
   242→    container.exec([ "chmod", "600", "/etc/wetty/key" ])
   243→  end
   244→
   245→  def write_traefik_config(sandbox)
   246→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
   247→
   248→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   249→    id = sandbox.id
   250→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   251→
   252→    rule = if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   253→      "HostRegexp(`.+`) && PathPrefix(`/terminal/#{id}/wetty`)"
   254→    else
   255→      "Host(`#{host}`) && PathPrefix(`/terminal/#{id}/wetty`)"
   256→    end
   257→
   258→    config = {
   259→      "http" => {
   260→        "routers" => {
   261→          "terminal-#{id}" => {
   262→            "rule" => rule,
   263→            "service" => "terminal-#{id}",
   264→            "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
   265→            "tls" => tls_config,
   266→            "middlewares" => [ "terminal-auth-#{id}" ],
   267→            "priority" => 100
   268→          }
   269→        },
   270→        "middlewares" => {
   271→          "terminal-auth-#{id}" => {
   272→            "forwardAuth" => {
   273→              "address" => "http://sandcastle-web:80/terminal/auth",
   274→              "trustForwardHeader" => true
   275→            }
   276→          }
   277→        },
   278→        "services" => {
   279→          "terminal-#{id}" => {
   280→            "loadBalancer" => {
   281→              "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{container_name}:3000" } ]
   282→            }
   283→          }
   284→        }
   285→      }
   286→    }
   287→
   288→    File.write(File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{id}.yml"), config.to_yaml)
   289→  end
   290→
   291→  def tls_config
   292→    if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   293→      {}
   294→    else
   295→      { "certResolver" => "letsencrypt" }
   296→    end
   297→  end
   298→
   299→  def delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
   300→    path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{sandbox.id}.yml")
   301→    File.delete(path) if File.exist?(path)
   302→  end
   303→
   304→  def remove_pubkey(sandbox)
   305→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   306→
   307→    begin
   308→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   309→      username = sandbox.user.name
   310→      marker = "wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}"
   311→      # Use grep -vF (fixed string) to avoid regex injection via sed
   312→      container.exec([
   313→        "sh", "-c",
   314→        "grep -vF '#{marker}' /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys > /tmp/ak_clean && " \
   315→        "mv /tmp/ak_clean /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys || true"
   316→      ])
   317→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError, Docker::Error::DockerError
   318→      # Sandbox container gone or exec failed — best-effort
   319→    end
   320→  end
   321→end
   322→
```

> AGENT

Here's a summary of what was fixed:

**Changes made:**

1. **`app/services/terminal_manager.rb`** — Two fixes:
   - **Removed bind mount for SSH key** — replaced `"Binds" => ["key_path:/etc/wetty/key:ro"]` with `copy_key_to_container()` that uses `container.exec` + base64 to copy the key after container start. Bind mounts fail when Rails runs inside a Docker container with a named volume for `/data` (Docker resolves paths against the host, not the container filesystem).
   - **No Binds in HostConfig** — WeTTY container is now created without volume mounts, key is injected after start.

2. **`docker-compose.local.yml`** — Two additions:
   - `SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE: selfsigned` env var so Traefik configs use `HostRegexp` instead of hostname matching
   - `sandcastle-web` external network so Rails auto-joins the Traefik/WeTTY network on compose up

**Verified working:**
- `TerminalManager.new.open(sandbox:)` completes successfully
- SSH key copied with correct permissions (0600)
- SSH from WeTTY → sandbox works (`echo connected`)
- Traefik → forwardAuth → WeTTY page serves 200
- Socket.IO polling endpoint works
- All 62 tests pass (0 failures)

> AGENT

Clear — the `TerminalController#open` redirects to `http://localhost:3000/terminal/7/wetty`, which hits Rails directly. Rails has no route for that GET path, so it's 404. The terminal page is served by the WeTTY container through Traefik on port 8443, not through Rails on port 3000.

The fix: redirect to the Traefik URL instead of a relative path. Let me update the controller.

> AGENT

The problem is clear: `TerminalController#open` redirects to `/terminal/7/wetty` — a relative path that stays on the same host:port. When the user is on `http://localhost:3000` (Rails direct), this 404s because Rails has no route for it. The path only works through Traefik on port 8443.

The fix: make the redirect build a full URL pointing to the Traefik endpoint. I'll use a `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL` env var to keep it configurable.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Wg37NuMvuUk7VWschFRfRF
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/terminal_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "  def open\n    sandbox = if Current.user.admin?\n      Sandbox.active.find(params[:id])\n    else\n      Current.user.sandboxes.active.find(params[:id])\n    end\n\n    url = TerminalManager.new.open(sandbox: sandbox)\n    redirect_to url, allow_other_host: false, status: :see_other\n  rescue TerminalManager::Error => e\n    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message\n  end",
  "new_string": "  def open\n    sandbox = if Current.user.admin?\n      Sandbox.active.find(params[:id])\n    else\n      Current.user.sandboxes.active.find(params[:id])\n    end\n\n    path = TerminalManager.new.open(sandbox: sandbox)\n    redirect_to terminal_redirect_url(path), allow_other_host: true, status: :see_other\n  rescue TerminalManager::Error => e\n    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message\n  end"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/terminal_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "  # Called by Traefik forwardAuth — returns status code only, no body/redirect",
  "new_string": "  private\n\n  # Build the full terminal URL. In production, Traefik is the entry point\n  # so a relative path works. In local dev (selfsigned TLS), Rails may be\n  # accessed directly on a different port, so we need an absolute URL.\n  def terminal_redirect_url(path)\n    base = ENV[\"SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL\"]\n    base ? \"#{base}#{path}\" : path\n  end\n\n  public\n\n  # Called by Traefik forwardAuth — returns status code only, no body/redirect"
}
```

   193→  def create_wetty_container(sandbox:, user:, key_dir:)
   194→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   195→
   196→    # Remove any existing container with this name
   197→    begin
   198→      old = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
   199→      old.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
   200→      old.delete(force: true)
   201→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   202→      # No existing container
   203→    end
   204→
   205→    container = Docker::Container.create(
   206→      "name" => container_name,
   207→      "Image" => WETTY_IMAGE,
   208→      "Env" => [
   209→        "SSHHOST=#{sandbox.full_name}",
   210→        "SSHPORT=22",
   211→        "SSHUSER=#{user.name}",
   212→        "SSHAUTH=publickey",
   213→        "SSHKEY=/etc/wetty/key",
   214→        "BASE=/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty"
   215→      ],
   216→      "HostConfig" => {
   217→        "NetworkMode" => NETWORK_NAME,
   218→        "RestartPolicy" => { "Name" => "no" },
   219→        "Memory" => 128 * 1024 * 1024, # 128MB
   220→        "NanoCpus" => 500_000_000 # 0.5 CPU
   221→      },
   222→      "Labels" => {
   223→        "sandcastle.sandbox_id" => sandbox.id.to_s,
   224→        "sandcastle.role" => "wetty"
   225→      }
   226→    )
   227→
   228→    container.start
   229→    copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)
   230→    container
   231→  end
   232→
   233→  # Copy the SSH private key into the WeTTY container via exec + base64.
   234→  # This avoids bind-mount path issues when Rails runs inside a container
   235→  # with a Docker volume for /data (the host path doesn't match).
   236→  def copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)
   237→    key_content = File.read(File.join(key_dir, "key"))
   238→    encoded = Base64.strict_encode64(key_content)
   239→
   240→    container.exec([ "mkdir", "-p", "/etc/wetty" ])
   241→    container.exec([ "sh", "-c", "echo #{encoded} | base64 -d > /etc/wetty/key" ])
   242→    container.exec([ "chmod", "600", "/etc/wetty/key" ])

> AGENT

Committed on the `wetty` branch:

- **`e3aea95`** — `feat: Use tmux sessions for WeTTY terminal connections`

This switches WeTTY from individual SSH env vars to a single `COMMAND` with `tmux new-session -A -s main`, so terminal reconnects resume the existing session.

> AGENT

`TERM` is empty in the WeTTY container environment. That's the likely cause — when SSH runs without a proper `TERM`, tmux can't initialize and says "bad terminal". Let me verify by testing SSH from within the WeTTY container.

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01DM62cU1VhxKxyTKmCcS2Gg

  "command": "docker exec sc-wetty-thies-thieso2 sh -c 'TERM= ssh -p 22 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR -i /etc/wetty/key thies@thies-thieso2 -t \"tmux new-session -A -s test\" 2>&1' || true",
  "description": "Test SSH + tmux without TERM set",
  "timeout": 10000
}

import isUndefined from 'lodash/isUndefined.js';
import pty from 'node-pty';
import { logger as getLogger } from '../shared/logger.js';
import { xterm } from './shared/xterm.js';
import { envVersionOr } from './spawn/env.js';
import { tinybuffer, FlowControlServer } from './flowcontrol.js';
export async function spawn(socket, args) {
    const logger = getLogger();
    const version = await envVersionOr(0);
    const cmd = version >= 9 ? ['-S', ...args] : args;
    logger.debug('Spawning PTY', { cmd });
    const term = pty.spawn('/usr/bin/env', cmd, xterm);
    const { pid } = term;
    const address = args[0] === 'ssh' ? args[1] : 'localhost';
    logger.info('Process Started on behalf of user', { pid, address });
    socket.emit('login');
    term.on('exit', (code) => {
        logger.info('Process exited', { code, pid });
        socket.emit('logout');
        socket
            .removeAllListeners('disconnect')
            .removeAllListeners('resize')
            .removeAllListeners('input');
    });
    const send = tinybuffer(socket, 2, 524288);
    const fcServer = new FlowControlServer();
    term.on('data', (data) => {
        send(data);
        if (fcServer.account(data.length)) {
            term.pause();
        }
    });
    socket
        .on('resize', ({ cols, rows }) => {
        term.resize(cols, rows);
    })
        .on('input', input => {
        if (!isUndefined(term))
            term.write(input);
    })
        .on('disconnect', () => {
        term.kill();
        logger.info('Process exited', { code: 0, pid });
    })
        .on('commit', size => {
        if (fcServer.commit(size)) {
            term.resume();
        }
    });
}
//# sourceMappingURL=spawn.js.map
```

import _ from "../../web_modules/pkg/lodash.js";
import {verifyPrompt} from "./disconnect/verify.js";
import {overlay} from "./disconnect/elements.js";
export function disconnect(reason) {
  if (_.isNull(overlay))
    return;
  overlay.style.display = "block";
  const msg = document.getElementById("msg");
  if (!_.isUndefined(reason) && !_.isNull(msg))
    msg.innerHTML = reason;
  window.removeEventListener("beforeunload", verifyPrompt, false);
}
```

import { isDev } from '../../shared/env.js';
const jsFiles = isDev ? ['dev', 'wetty'] : ['wetty'];
const cssFiles = ['styles', 'options', 'overlay', 'terminal'];
const render = (title, favicon, css, js, configUrl) => `<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="utf8">
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=edge">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no">
    <link rel="icon" type="image/x-icon" href="${favicon}">
    <title>${title}</title>
    ${css.map(file => `<link rel="stylesheet" href="${file}" />`).join('\n')}
  </head>
  <body>
    <div id="overlay">
      <div class="error">
        <div id="msg"></div>
        <input type="button" onclick="location.reload();" value="reconnect" />
      </div>
    </div>
    <div id="options">
      <a class="toggler"
         href="#"
         alt="Toggle options"
       ><i class="fas fa-cogs"></i></a>
      <iframe class="editor" src="${configUrl}"></iframe>
    </div>
    <div id="terminal"></div>
    ${js
    .map(file => `<script type="module" src="${file}"></script>`)
    .join('\n')}
  </body>
</html>`;
export const html = (base, title) => (_req, res) => {
    res.send(render(title, `${base}/favicon.ico`, cssFiles.map(css => `${base}/assets/css/${css}.css`), jsFiles.map(js => `${base}/client/${js}.js`), `${base}/assets/xterm_config/index.html`));
};
//# sourceMappingURL=html.js.map

import _ from "../web_modules/pkg/lodash.js";
import {dom, library} from "../web_modules/pkg/@fortawesome/fontawesome-svg-core.js";
import {faCogs} from "../web_modules/pkg/@fortawesome/free-solid-svg-icons.js";
import {FileDownloader} from "./wetty/download.js";
import {disconnect} from "./wetty/disconnect.js";
import {mobileKeyboard} from "./wetty/mobile.js";
import {overlay} from "./wetty/disconnect/elements.js";
import {socket} from "./wetty/socket.js";
import {verifyPrompt} from "./wetty/disconnect/verify.js";
import {terminal} from "./wetty/term.js";
import {FlowControlClient} from "./wetty/flowcontrol.js";
library.add(faCogs);
dom.watch();
function onResize(term) {
  return function resize() {
    term.resizeTerm();
  };
}
socket.on("connect", () => {
  const term = terminal(socket);
  if (_.isUndefined(term))
    return;
  if (!_.isNull(overlay))
    overlay.style.display = "none";
  window.addEventListener("beforeunload", verifyPrompt, false);
  window.addEventListener("resize", onResize(term), false);
  term.resizeTerm();
  term.focus();
  mobileKeyboard();
  const fileDownloader = new FileDownloader();
  const fcClient = new FlowControlClient();
  term.onData((data) => {
    socket.emit("input", data);
  });
  term.onResize((size) => {
    socket.emit("resize", size);
  });
  socket.on("data", (data) => {
    const remainingData = fileDownloader.buffer(data);
    const downloadLength = data.length - remainingData.length;
    if (downloadLength && fcClient.needsCommit(downloadLength)) {
      socket.emit("commit", fcClient.ackBytes);
    }
    if (remainingData) {
      if (fcClient.needsCommit(remainingData.length)) {
        term.write(remainingData, () => socket.emit("commit", fcClient.ackBytes));
      } else {
        term.write(remainingData);
      }
    }
  }).on("login", () => {
    term.writeln("");
    term.resizeTerm();
  }).on("logout", disconnect).on("disconnect", disconnect).on("error", (err) => {
    if (err)
      disconnect(err);
  });
});
```

> AGENT

Now I see it clearly. The first Terminal click at `05:08:09` completed in 364ms and redirected. But the WeTTY page only loaded at `05:08:14.820` — from the SECOND click. The first redirect hit Traefik before the config was loaded, so it fell through to Rails (404).

The fix is to add a small delay or wait for Traefik to pick up the config before redirecting. Let me verify the Traefik config detection timing:

There are actually TWO issues:

1. **Race condition**: Traefik hasn't loaded the config when the browser follows the redirect (~0.5s gap)
2. **SSH exits after 3 seconds**: The WeTTY WebSocket disconnects, killing the SSH process

> AGENT

Found a critical clue! The authorized_keys in sandbox 10 has sandbox 11's WeTTY key but NOT sandbox 10's:

```
ssh-rsa ... thies@thieso.net         ← user's key
ssh-ed25519 ... wetty-thies-thieso2  ← sandbox 11's WeTTY key (WRONG!)
```

Both sandboxes share the same `/home/thies` via bind mount. The entrypoint for sandbox 11 probably overwrote the authorized_keys. Let me check:

> TOOL

     4→USERNAME="${SANDCASTLE_USER:-sandbox}"

     6→
     7→# Create user if not exists
     8→if ! id "$USERNAME" &>/dev/null; then
     9→    useradd -m -s /bin/bash -G sudo,docker "$USERNAME"
    10→    echo "$USERNAME ALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD:ALL" >> /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle

    31→USER_LOCAL_BIN="/home/$USERNAME/.local/bin"

    34→    if [ ! -f "$USER_LOCAL_BIN/$tool" ] && [ -f "/opt/sandcastle/bin/$tool" ]; then
    35→        cp "/opt/sandcastle/bin/$tool" "$USER_LOCAL_BIN/$tool"
    36→    fi
    37→done

    46→    ETH0_MTU=$(ip link show eth0 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'mtu \K[0-9]+' || echo 1500)
    47→    dockerd --storage-driver=overlay2 --mtu="$ETH0_MTU" &>/var/log/dockerd.log &

    54→    done

> AGENT

Found it! Line 18: `echo "$SSH_KEY" > "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"` — the entrypoint **overwrites** (not appends) authorized_keys. Since all sandboxes for the same user share `/home/thies` via bind mount, creating sandbox 11 overwrites sandbox 10's authorized_keys, wiping the WeTTY key.

But this doesn't explain the 3-second exit on the FIRST try (before sandbox 11 existed). Let me check if there's an issue with the first SSH attempt timing:

> AGENT

OK, I've found the root causes. There are two bugs:

**Bug 1 - Traefik config race**: `write_traefik_config` is the LAST step in `open()`. By the time the browser follows the redirect, Traefik hasn't loaded the config yet → 404.

**Bug 2 - Entrypoint overwrites authorized_keys**: `entrypoint.sh` line 18 uses `>` (overwrite) not `>>`. Since all sandboxes for the same user share `/home/{user}` via bind mount, creating a second sandbox wipes the first sandbox's WeTTY key from authorized_keys.

There's also a **Bug 3** - `remove_pubkey` substring match: `grep -vF 'wetty-thies-thieso'` would also remove `wetty-thies-thieso2` since it's a substring.

Let me fix all three:

> TOOL

tool_use Read
id: toolu_011SJ3jtZLS3q3hvAiV9mgJF
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/terminal_manager.rb"
}
```

tool_result
id: toolu_011SJ3jtZLS3q3hvAiV9mgJF
```
     1→class TerminalManager
     2→  DATA_DIR = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR", "/data")
     3→  WETTY_IMAGE = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_WETTY_IMAGE", "wettyoss/wetty:latest")
     4→  NETWORK_NAME = "sandcastle-web"
     5→  DYNAMIC_DIR = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "dynamic")
     6→
     7→  class Error < StandardError; end
     8→
     9→  # Opens a web terminal for the given sandbox.
    10→  # Returns the URL path to the WeTTY session.
    11→  def open(sandbox:)
    12→    raise Error, "Sandbox is not running" unless sandbox.status == "running"
    13→    raise Error, "Sandbox has no container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
    14→
    15→    user = sandbox.user
    16→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
    17→
    18→    # Idempotent: if WeTTY container already running, return URL
    19→    if container_running?(container_name)
    20→      return wetty_url(sandbox)
    21→    end
    22→
    23→    pull_image
    24→    ensure_network
    25→    connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
    26→    key_dir = generate_keypair(sandbox)
    27→    inject_pubkey(sandbox, key_dir)
    28→    create_wetty_container(sandbox: sandbox, user: user, key_dir: key_dir)
    29→    write_traefik_config(sandbox)
    30→
    31→    wetty_url(sandbox)
    32→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    33→    raise Error, "Failed to open terminal: #{e.message}"
    34→  rescue SystemCallError => e
    35→    raise Error, "Failed to open terminal: #{e.message}"
    36→  end
    37→
    38→  # Closes the web terminal for the given sandbox.
    39→  def close(sandbox:)
    40→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
    41→
    42→    # Stop and remove WeTTY container
    43→    begin
    44→      container = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
    45→      container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    46→      container.delete(force: true)
    47→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
    48→      # Already gone
    49→    end
    50→
    51→    # Delete Traefik config
    52→    delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
    53→
    54→    # Clean up key directory
    55→    key_dir = key_dir_path(sandbox)
    56→    FileUtils.rm_rf(key_dir)
    57→
    58→    # Best-effort: remove pubkey from sandbox authorized_keys
    59→    remove_pubkey(sandbox)
    60→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    61→    Rails.logger.error("TerminalManager: close failed for #{sandbox.full_name}: #{e.message}")
    62→  end
    63→
    64→  # Returns true if the WeTTY container is running for this sandbox.
    65→  def active?(sandbox:)
    66→    container_running?(wetty_container_name(sandbox))
    67→  end
    68→
    69→  # Removes orphaned WeTTY containers whose sandbox no longer exists or is not running.
    70→  def cleanup_orphaned
    71→    Docker::Container.all(all: true).each do |container|
    72→      name = container.info.dig("Names")&.first&.delete_prefix("/")
    73→      next unless name&.start_with?("sc-wetty-")
    74→
    75→      labels = container.info["Labels"] || {}
    76→      sandbox_id = labels["sandcastle.sandbox_id"]&.to_i
    77→
    78→      sandbox = sandbox_id ? Sandbox.find_by(id: sandbox_id) : nil
    79→      should_remove = sandbox.nil? || sandbox.status != "running"
    80→
    81→      if should_remove
    82→        container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    83→        container.delete(force: true)
    84→        Rails.logger.info("TerminalManager: removed orphaned WeTTY container #{name}")
    85→
    86→        # Clean up Traefik config and keys if we have a sandbox_id
    87→        if sandbox_id
    88→          config_path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{sandbox_id}.yml")
    89→          File.delete(config_path) if File.exist?(config_path)
    90→        end
    91→
    92→        # Extract full_name from container name and validate format
    93→        full_name = name.delete_prefix("sc-wetty-")
    94→        unless full_name.match?(/\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]+-[a-z][a-z0-9_-]*\z/)
    95→          Rails.logger.warn("TerminalManager: skipping suspicious container name: #{name}")
    96→          next
    97→        end
    98→
    99→        key_dir = File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty", full_name)
   100→        expected_parent = File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty")
   101→        unless File.expand_path(key_dir).start_with?("#{File.expand_path(expected_parent)}/")
   102→          Rails.logger.warn("TerminalManager: path traversal attempt detected: #{key_dir}")
   103→          next
   104→        end
   105→
   106→        FileUtils.rm_rf(key_dir) if Dir.exist?(key_dir)
   107→      end
   108→    end
   109→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   110→    Rails.logger.error("TerminalManager: orphan cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
   111→  end
   112→
   113→  private
   114→
   115→  def wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   116→    "sc-wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}"
   117→  end
   118→
   119→  def wetty_url(sandbox)
   120→    "/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty"
   121→  end

   123→  def key_dir_path(sandbox)

   125→  end
   126→
   127→  def container_running?(name)

   129→    container.json.dig("State", "Running") == true

   131→    false
   132→  end
   133→
   134→  def pull_image
   135→    Docker::Image.get(WETTY_IMAGE)
   136→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   137→    Docker::Image.create("fromImage" => WETTY_IMAGE)
   138→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError

   140→  end
   141→
   142→  def ensure_network
   143→    Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   144→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   145→    Docker::Network.create(NETWORK_NAME, "Driver" => "bridge")
   146→  end
   147→
   148→  def connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
   149→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   150→
   151→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   152→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   153→
   154→    networks = container.json.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks") || {}
   155→    return if networks.key?(NETWORK_NAME)
   156→
   157→    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)
   158→  end
   159→
   160→  def generate_keypair(sandbox)
   161→    key_dir = key_dir_path(sandbox)
   162→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(key_dir, mode: 0o700)
   163→
   164→    key_path = File.join(key_dir, "key")
   165→    # Remove old keys if they exist
   166→    FileUtils.rm_f(key_path)
   167→    FileUtils.rm_f("#{key_path}.pub")
   168→
   169→    system("ssh-keygen", "-t", "ed25519", "-f", key_path, "-N", "", "-q", "-C", "wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}",
   170→      exception: true)
   171→
   172→    # Ensure private key is only readable by owner
   173→    File.chmod(0o600, key_path)
   174→
   175→    key_dir
   176→  end
   177→
   178→  def inject_pubkey(sandbox, key_dir)
   179→    pubkey = File.read(File.join(key_dir, "key.pub")).strip
   180→    username = sandbox.user.name
   181→
   182→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   183→
   184→    # Write pubkey via base64 to avoid any shell-injection risk.
   185→    # The stdin: approach hangs because docker-api never sends EOF to cat.
   186→    encoded = Base64.strict_encode64("#{pubkey}\n")
   187→    container.exec([ "mkdir", "-p", "/home/#{username}/.ssh" ])
   188→    container.exec([ "sh", "-c", "echo #{encoded} | base64 -d >> /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys" ])
   189→    container.exec([ "chown", "-R", "#{username}:#{username}", "/home/#{username}/.ssh" ])
   190→    container.exec([ "chmod", "600", "/home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys" ])
   191→  end
   192→
   193→  def create_wetty_container(sandbox:, user:, key_dir:)
   194→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   195→
   196→    # Remove any existing container with this name
   197→    begin
   198→      old = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
   199→      old.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
   200→      old.delete(force: true)
   201→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   202→      # No existing container
   203→    end
   204→
   205→    ssh_command = [
   206→      "ssh", "-p", "22",
   207→      "-o", "StrictHostKeyChecking=no",
   208→      "-o", "UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null",
   209→      "-o", "LogLevel=ERROR",
   210→      "-i", "/etc/wetty/key",
   211→      "#{user.name}@#{sandbox.full_name}",
   212→      "-t", "tmux new-session -A -s main"
   213→    ].join(" ")
   214→
   215→    container = Docker::Container.create(
   216→      "name" => container_name,
   217→      "Image" => WETTY_IMAGE,
   218→      "Env" => [
   219→        "COMMAND=#{ssh_command}",
   220→        "BASE=/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty"
   221→      ],
   222→      "HostConfig" => {
   223→        "NetworkMode" => NETWORK_NAME,
   224→        "RestartPolicy" => { "Name" => "no" },
   225→        "Memory" => 128 * 1024 * 1024, # 128MB
   226→        "NanoCpus" => 500_000_000 # 0.5 CPU
   227→      },
   228→      "Labels" => {
   229→        "sandcastle.sandbox_id" => sandbox.id.to_s,
   230→        "sandcastle.role" => "wetty"
   231→      }
   232→    )
   233→
   234→    container.start
   235→    copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)
   236→    container
   237→  end
   238→
   239→  # Copy the SSH private key into the WeTTY container via exec + base64.
   240→  # This avoids bind-mount path issues when Rails runs inside a container
   241→  # with a Docker volume for /data (the host path doesn't match).
   242→  def copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)
   243→    key_content = File.read(File.join(key_dir, "key"))
   244→    encoded = Base64.strict_encode64(key_content)
   245→
   246→    container.exec([ "mkdir", "-p", "/etc/wetty" ])
   247→    container.exec([ "sh", "-c", "echo #{encoded} | base64 -d > /etc/wetty/key" ])
   248→    container.exec([ "chmod", "600", "/etc/wetty/key" ])
   249→  end
   250→
   251→  def write_traefik_config(sandbox)
   252→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
   253→
   254→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   255→    id = sandbox.id
   256→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   257→
   258→    rule = if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   259→      "HostRegexp(`.+`) && PathPrefix(`/terminal/#{id}/wetty`)"
   260→    else
   261→      "Host(`#{host}`) && PathPrefix(`/terminal/#{id}/wetty`)"
   262→    end
   263→
   264→    config = {
   265→      "http" => {
   266→        "routers" => {
   267→          "terminal-#{id}" => {
   268→            "rule" => rule,
   269→            "service" => "terminal-#{id}",
   270→            "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
   271→            "tls" => tls_config,
   272→            "middlewares" => [ "terminal-auth-#{id}" ],
   273→            "priority" => 100
   274→          }
   275→        },
   276→        "middlewares" => {
   277→          "terminal-auth-#{id}" => {
   278→            "forwardAuth" => {
   279→              "address" => "http://sandcastle-web:80/terminal/auth",
   280→              "trustForwardHeader" => true
   281→            }
   282→          }
   283→        },
   284→        "services" => {
   285→          "terminal-#{id}" => {
   286→            "loadBalancer" => {
   287→              "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{container_name}:3000" } ]
   288→            }
   289→          }
   290→        }
   291→      }
   292→    }
   293→
   294→    File.write(File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{id}.yml"), config.to_yaml)
   295→  end
   296→
   297→  def tls_config
   298→    if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   299→      {}
   300→    else
   301→      { "certResolver" => "letsencrypt" }
   302→    end
   303→  end
   304→
   305→  def delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
   306→    path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{sandbox.id}.yml")
   307→    File.delete(path) if File.exist?(path)
   308→  end
   309→
   310→  def remove_pubkey(sandbox)
   311→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   312→
   313→    begin
   314→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   315→      username = sandbox.user.name
   316→      marker = "wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}"
   317→      # Use grep -vF (fixed string) to avoid regex injection via sed
   318→      container.exec([
   319→        "sh", "-c",
   320→        "grep -vF '#{marker}' /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys > /tmp/ak_clean && " \
   321→        "mv /tmp/ak_clean /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys || true"
   322→      ])
   323→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError, Docker::Error::DockerError
   324→      # Sandbox container gone or exec failed — best-effort
   325→    end
   326→  end
   327→end
   328→
```

> AGENT

Now let me apply all three fixes:

**Fix 1**: Move `write_traefik_config` before `create_wetty_container` to give Traefik time to load the config while the container is being created.

**Fix 2**: Fix `entrypoint.sh` to not clobber existing keys in the shared authorized_keys file.

**Fix 3**: Fix `remove_pubkey` to use exact line matching (add a trailing space+marker boundary).

> TOOL

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/terminal_manager.rb",
  "old_string": "    pull_image\n    ensure_network\n    connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)\n    key_dir = generate_keypair(sandbox)\n    inject_pubkey(sandbox, key_dir)\n    create_wetty_container(sandbox: sandbox, user: user, key_dir: key_dir)\n    write_traefik_config(sandbox)\n\n    wetty_url(sandbox)",
  "new_string": "    pull_image\n    ensure_network\n    connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)\n\n    # Write Traefik config early so it has time to detect the new route\n    # while we set up keypairs and start the WeTTY container.\n    write_traefik_config(sandbox)\n\n    key_dir = generate_keypair(sandbox)\n    inject_pubkey(sandbox, key_dir)\n    create_wetty_container(sandbox: sandbox, user: user, key_dir: key_dir)\n\n    # Give Traefik a moment to load the dynamic config before redirecting.\n    sleep 1\n\n    wetty_url(sandbox)"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/terminal_manager.rb",
  "old_string": "      marker = \"wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}\"\n      # Use grep -vF (fixed string) to avoid regex injection via sed\n      container.exec([\n        \"sh\", \"-c\",\n        \"grep -vF '#{marker}' /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys > /tmp/ak_clean && \" \\\n        \"mv /tmp/ak_clean /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys || true\"\n      ])",
  "new_string": "      marker = \" wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}\\n\"\n      # Match \" wetty-{name}\\n\" exactly to avoid substring collisions\n      # (e.g. \"wetty-thies-foo\" must not also remove \"wetty-thies-foobar\").\n      # Use grep -vF (fixed string) to avoid regex injection via sed.\n      container.exec([\n        \"sh\", \"-c\",\n        \"grep -vF '#{marker.chomp}' /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys > /tmp/ak_clean && \" \\\n        \"mv /tmp/ak_clean /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys || true\"\n      ])"
}

> AGENT

Wait, the `-F` (fixed string) approach still has the substring issue. I need to use regex with `$` anchor for exact end-of-line matching. Sandbox names are safe for regex (only `[a-z0-9_-]`):

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01S5jScy91vxiq93vhJtajaJ
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/terminal_manager.rb",
  "old_string": "      marker = \" wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}\\n\"\n      # Match \" wetty-{name}\\n\" exactly to avoid substring collisions\n      # (e.g. \"wetty-thies-foo\" must not also remove \"wetty-thies-foobar\").\n      # Use grep -vF (fixed string) to avoid regex injection via sed.\n      container.exec([\n        \"sh\", \"-c\",\n        \"grep -vF '#{marker.chomp}' /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys > /tmp/ak_clean && \" \\\n        \"mv /tmp/ak_clean /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys || true\"\n      ])",
  "new_string": "      # Match end-of-line to avoid substring collisions\n      # (e.g. \"wetty-user-foo\" must not also remove \"wetty-user-foobar\").\n      # Sandbox names are [a-z0-9_-] only, so safe for regex.\n      marker = \"wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}$\"\n      container.exec([\n        \"sh\", \"-c\",\n        \"grep -v '#{marker}' /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys > /tmp/ak_clean && \" \\\n        \"mv /tmp/ak_clean /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys || true\"\n      ])"
}
```

Now fix the entrypoint to not clobber existing authorized_keys:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01CTTutpMu5nGqHzCXiggjj4
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh",
  "old_string": "# Set up SSH authorized keys\nif [ -n \"$SSH_KEY\" ]; then\n    SSH_DIR=\"/home/$USERNAME/.ssh\"\n    mkdir -p \"$SSH_DIR\"\n    echo \"$SSH_KEY\" > \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\"\n    chmod 700 \"$SSH_DIR\"\n    chmod 600 \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\"\n    chown -R \"$USERNAME:$USERNAME\" \"$SSH_DIR\"\nfi",
  "new_string": "# Set up SSH authorized keys (append if not already present, preserving\n# any WeTTY keys that may have been injected for other sandboxes sharing\n# this user's home directory via bind mount).\nif [ -n \"$SSH_KEY\" ]; then\n    SSH_DIR=\"/home/$USERNAME/.ssh\"\n    mkdir -p \"$SSH_DIR\"\n    if [ -f \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\" ]; then\n        grep -qF \"$SSH_KEY\" \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\" || echo \"$SSH_KEY\" >> \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\"\n    else\n        echo \"$SSH_KEY\" > \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\"\n    fi\n    chmod 700 \"$SSH_DIR\"\n    chmod 600 \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\"\n    chown -R \"$USERNAME:$USERNAME\" \"$SSH_DIR\"\nfi"
}
```

> AGENT

Still not connecting from this end. The MCP connection between Claude Code and the Chrome extension may need a restart of Claude Code itself, or the extension may need to be reconnected from the extension popup.

In the meantime, here's a summary of what I've already fixed. You can test manually:

**3 bugs fixed:**

1. **Traefik config race** (`terminal_manager.rb`): Moved `write_traefik_config` before container creation + added `sleep 1` to give Traefik time to detect the route before the browser redirects.

2. **Entrypoint overwrites authorized_keys** (`images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh`): Changed from `echo "$SSH_KEY" >` (overwrite) to `grep -qF` + `>>` (append only if not present). This preserves WeTTY keys when multiple sandboxes share the same user home via bind mount.

3. **remove_pubkey substring match** (`terminal_manager.rb`): Changed `grep -vF 'wetty-thies-foo'` to `grep -v 'wetty-thies-foo$'` with end-of-line anchor, so removing sandbox `foo`'s key doesn't also remove `foobar`'s key.

**Both images are rebuilt** (`sandcastle-sandbox:latest` and `sandcastle:local`), Rails container is restarted, and old WeTTY containers are cleaned up.

**To test manually:**
1. Go to `https://localhost:8443`
2. Create a new sandbox (or use an existing one)
3. Click Terminal — it should work on the **first try** now

Want me to keep trying Chrome, or would you like to test and report back?

+    fi
     chmod 700 "$SSH_DIR"

> AGENT

The auth system already has `return_to_after_authenticating` support. The issue is that the Traefik `forwardAuth` endpoint (`/terminal/auth`) returns a bare `401` status — Traefik passes that to the browser as-is. I need to make it redirect to the login page instead, with a return_to URL pointing back to the terminal.

Returns 200 to allow, or redirects to\n  # login (which Traefik passes through to the browser).\n  def auth\n    forwarded_uri = request.headers[\"X-Forwarded-Uri\"] || \"\"\n    match = forwarded_uri.match(%r{/terminal/(\\d+)/wetty})\n    head(:unauthorized) and return unless match\n\n    session_record = find_session_by_cookie\n    unless session_record\n      # Build the original terminal URL from Traefik's forwarded headers\n      # so the user returns here after logging in.\n      proto = request.headers[\"X-Forwarded-Proto\"] || \"https\"\n      host  = request.headers[\"X-Forwarded-Host\"] || request.host_with_port\n      original_url = \"#{proto}://#{host}#{forwarded_uri}\"\n      session[:return_to_after_authenticating] = original_url\n      redirect_to new_session_url, allow_other_host: true\n      return\n    end\n\n    user = session_record.user\n    sandbox = Sandbox.active.find_by(id: match[1].to_i)\n    head(:unauthorized) and return unless sandbox && (sandbox.user_id == user.id || user.admin?)\n\n    head :ok\n  end"

    56→      original_url = "#{proto}://#{host}#{forwarded_uri}"
    57→      session[:return_to_after_authenticating] = original_url
    58→      redirect_to new_session_url, allow_other_host: true
    59→      return
    60→    end
    61→
    62→    user = session_record.user
    63→    sandbox = Sandbox.active.find_by(id: match[1].to_i)
    64→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless sandbox && (sandbox.user_id == user.id || user.admin?)
    65→
    66→    head :ok
    67→  end
    68→end
    69→
```

   182→    TerminalManager.define_singleton_method(:new) { stub }

     1→# Sandcastle
     2→
     3→Self-hosted shared Docker sandbox platform. Each user gets isolated [Sysbox](https://github.com/nestybox/sysbox) containers with SSH access and a full Docker daemon inside.
     4→
     5→## Architecture
     6→
     7→```mermaid
     8→flowchart TB
     9→    CLI["Go CLI\n(sandcastle)"] --> API["Rails API\n(Bearer token)"]
    10→    Browser["Web Dashboard"] --> Web["Rails Web\n(Session auth)"]
    11→    API --> SM["SandboxManager"]
    12→    Web --> SM
    13→    SM --> Docker["Docker + Sysbox"]
    14→    Docker --> S1["Sandbox 1\n(SSH + DinD)"]
    15→    Docker --> S2["Sandbox 2\n(SSH + DinD)"]
    16→    SM --> TS["TailscaleManager"]
    17→    TS --> TSC["Tailscale\nSidecars"]
    18→```
    19→
    20→- **Backend:** Rails 8.1 (Ruby 4.0), SQLite, Solid Queue/Cache/Cable
    21→- **CLI:** Go + Cobra (`vendor/sandcastle-cli/`)
    22→- **Containers:** Docker + Sysbox (`sysbox-runc`) for sandboxes
    23→- **Auth:** Session-based (web) + API tokens (`sc_` prefix, bcrypt)
    24→- **Sandbox image:** Ubuntu 24.04 with Docker-in-Docker, SSH, dev tools
    25→
    26→## Getting Started
    27→
    28→### Prerequisites
    29→
    30→- Ruby 4.0
    31→- Docker with [Sysbox](https://github.com/nestybox/sysbox) runtime
    32→- Go 1.22+ (for CLI development)
    33→
    34→### Development
    35→
    36→```bash
    37→bin/dev              # Start dev server (web + Tailwind watcher)
    38→bin/rails test       # Run tests
    39→bin/rubocop          # Lint
    40→bin/brakeman         # Security scan
    41→bin/ci               # Full CI suite
    42→```
    43→
    44→### CLI
    45→
    46→```bash
    47→cd vendor/sandcastle-cli && make build
    48→sandcastle create my-dev --home --data
    49→sandcastle list
    50→sandcastle ssh my-dev
    51→sandcastle destroy my-dev
    52→```
    53→
    54→## Deployment
    55→
    56→See [DEPLOY.md](DEPLOY.md) for registry setup, CI/CD pipeline, and deploy workflows.
    57→
    58→## License
    59→
    60→MIT
    61→
```
